While accuracy is not influenced by how we split the model in ordinary, state-of-the-art SFL, in this work we answer the questions above in the affirmative. Recent Hierarchical SFL (HSFL) architectures adopt a three-tier training structure consisting of clients, (local) aggregators, and a central server. In this architecture, the model is partitioned at two partitioning layers into three sub-models, which are executed across the three tiers. Despite their merits, HSFL architectures overlook the impact of the partitioning layers and client-to-aggregator assignments on accuracy, delay, and overhead. This work explicitly captures the impact of the partitioning layers and client-to-aggregator assignments on accuracy, delay and overhead by formulating a joint optimization problem. We prove that the problem is NP-hard and propose the first accuracy-aware heuristic algorithm that explicitly accounts for model accuracy, while remaining delay-efficient. Simulation results on public datasets show that our approach can improve accuracy by 3%, while reducing delay by 20% and overhead by 50%, compared to state-of-the-art SFL and HSFL schemes.

</details>


### [150] [Impermanent: A Live Benchmark for Temporal Generalization in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08707)
*Azul Garza,Renée Rosillo,Rodrigo Mendoza-Smith,David Salinas,Andrew Robert Williams,Arjun Ashok,Mononito Goswami,José Martín Juárez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Impermanent是一个用于时间序列预测的实时基准测试框架，通过连续更新的数据流对预测模型进行顺序评分，专注于评估时间鲁棒性、分布漂移和性能稳定性，而非静态测试集的单次准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测领域越来越多地使用预训练基础模型，这些模型声称具有广泛泛化能力，但现有评估协议存在局限性。静态训练-测试分割容易导致数据污染（基础模型可能无意中在测试数据上训练或使用测试分数进行模型选择），从而夸大性能表现。

Method: 引入Impermanent实时基准测试，在开放世界时间变化下评估预测模型，通过对连续更新的数据流进行顺序预测评分。具体实例化在GitHub开源活动上，使用按星标数排名前400的仓库，构建从问题开启、拉取请求开启、推送事件和新星标用户等时间序列，采用滚动窗口和每日更新进行评估，并建立标准化协议和排行榜。

Result: 提供了可重现、持续比较的基准测试框架，将评估重点从静态准确性转向持续性能，能够研究时间鲁棒性、分布漂移和性能稳定性。代码和实时仪表板已公开可用。

Conclusion: 通过将评估从静态准确性转向持续性能，Impermanent朝着评估时间序列预测中基础级泛化能力何时以及是否能够被有意义地声称迈出了具体一步。该框架为研究非平稳环境下的模型性能提供了更真实的评估方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in time-series forecasting increasingly rely on pre-trained foundation-style models. While these models often claim broad generalization, existing evaluation protocols provide limited evidence. Indeed, most current benchmarks use static train-test splits that can easily lead to contamination as foundation models can inadvertently train on test data or perform model selection using test scores, which can inflate performance. We introduce Impermanent, a live benchmark that evaluates forecasting models under open-world temporal change by scoring forecasts sequentially over time on continuously updated data streams, enabling the study of temporal robustness, distributional shift, and performance stability rather than one-off accuracy on a frozen test set. Impermanent is instantiated on GitHub open-source activity, providing a naturally live and highly non-stationary dataset shaped by releases, shifting contributor behavior, platform/tooling changes, and external events. We focus on the top 400 repositories by star count and construct time series from issues opened, pull requests opened, push events, and new stargazers, evaluated over a rolling window with daily updates, alongside standardized protocols and leaderboards for reproducible, ongoing comparison. By shifting evaluation from static accuracy to sustained performance, Impermanent takes a concrete step toward assessing when and whether foundation-level generalization in time-series forecasting can be meaningfully claimed. Code and a live dashboard are available at https://github.com/TimeCopilot/impermanent and https://impermanent.timecopilot.dev.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [pqRPKI: A Practical RPKI Architecture for the Post-Quantum Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06968)
*Weitong Li,Yuze Li,Taejoong Chung*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: pqRPKI 是一个后量子 RPKI 框架，使用多层 Merkle 树梯子替代传统签名，显著减少存储和验证开销，支持与现有 RSA 系统的双栈部署


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPKI 使用 RSA 签名保护互联网路由，但面临量子计算威胁。直接替换为后量子签名（如 Falcon）会因密钥和签名体积增大而显著增加带宽和 CPU 开销，特别是在长期双栈过渡期间

Method: 设计 pqRPKI 框架，将多层 Merkle 树梯子与 RPKI 对象配对，重新设计 RPKI manifest 和委托链，引入梯子引导的同步和批量验证工作流，使验证器能够自上而下定位差异并自下而上重建树

Result: pqRPKI 将存储库大小减少到平均 546.8 MB（比 Falcon/ML-DSA 小 65.5%/83.1%），完整周期验证时间降至 102.7 秒，端到端 PP 到路由器时间为 118.3 秒，支持每周期完整存储库验证的 2 分钟操作节奏。与现有 RPKI 存储库相比，RSA 双栈部署仅增加 3.4% 的大小开销

Conclusion: pqRPKI 提供了一个高效的后量子 RPKI 解决方案，通过 Merkle 树结构显著降低了存储和计算开销，同时保持与现有 RPKI 对象的兼容性，支持渐进式迁移路径，为量子安全互联网路由提供了实用框架

Abstract: The Resource Public Key Infrastructure (RPKI) secures Internet routing by binding IP prefixes to authorized Autonomous Systems, yet its RSA foundations are vulnerable to quantum adversaries. A naive swap to post-quantum (PQ) signatures (eg Falcon) is a poor fit for RPKI's bulk model: every relying party (RP) repeatedly fetches and validates the entire global repository, so larger keys and signatures inflate bandwidth and CPU cost, especially during a long dual-stack transition. We present pqRPKI , a post-quantum RPKI framework that pairs a multi-layer Merkle Tree Ladder (MTL) with RPKI objects, customized to relocate per-object verification material from certificates into the Manifest. To update RPKI for Merkle tree based schemes, pqRPKI redesign the RPKI manifest and delegation chain, introduces a ladder-guided sync and bulk-verification workflow that lets validators localize diffs top-down and rebuild trees bottom-up.
  pqRPKI also preserves current RPKI objects and encodings, supports both hosted and delegated operation, and provides an additive migration path that coexists with today's trust anchors for dual-stack deployment with little size overhead. Implemented as a working publication point (PP) and RPs, we show that pqRPKI reduces repository footprint to 546.8 MB on average (65.5%/83.1% smaller than Falcon/ML-DSA), cuts full-cycle validation to 102.7 s, and achieves 118.3 s end-to-end PP to Router time, enabling sub-2-minute operating cadences with full-repository validation each cycle. Dual-stack deployment with RSA only adds just 3.4% size overhead versus today's RPKI repositories.

</details>


### [152] [Scheduling Parallel Optical Circuit Switches for AI Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07373)
*Kevin Liang,Litao Qiao,Isaac Keslassy,Bill Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Spectra算法通过三步法优化并行光电路交换机的AI流量调度，显著降低调度完成时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练快速增长导致数据中心流量需求和能耗激增，光电路交换机作为高带宽、高能效的替代方案受到关注。然而，在具有不可忽略重配置延迟的并行光交换机上高效调度时变流量矩阵仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: Spectra算法采用三步法：1) 将流量需求矩阵D分解为最小加权置换集；2) 使用负载感知分配将这些置换调度到并行交换机；3) 通过受控置换分割均衡交换机间的负载不平衡。

Result: 在真实AI训练负载（GPT模型和Qwen MoE专家路由）以及标准基准测试上，Spectra显著优于基于最先进算法的基线：在GPT AI负载上平均减少1.4倍调度完成时间，在MoE AI负载上减少1.9倍，在标准基准上减少2.4倍。Spectra实现的完成时间始终接近新推导的下界。

Conclusion: Spectra算法有效解决了并行光电路交换机中AI流量调度的挑战，通过创新的分解、调度和均衡策略，显著提升了调度效率并接近理论最优性能。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI training has dramatically increased datacenter traffic demand and energy consumption, which has motivated renewed interest in optical circuit switches (OCSes) as a high-bandwidth, energy-efficient alternative for AI fabrics. Deploying multiple parallel OCSes is a leading alternative. However, efficiently scheduling time-varying traffic matrices across parallel optical switches with non-negligible reconfiguration delays remains an open challenge.
  We consider the problem of scheduling a single AI traffic demand matrix $D$ over $s$ parallel OCSes while minimizing the makespan under reconfiguration delay $δ$. Our algorithm Spectra relies on a three-step approach: Decompose $D$ into a minimal set of weighted permutations; Schedule these permutations across parallel switches using load-aware assignment; then Equalize the imbalanced loads on the switches via controlled permutation splitting. Evaluated on realistic AI training workloads (GPT model and Qwen MoE expert routing) as well as standard benchmarks, Spectra vastly outperforms a baseline based on state-of-the-art algorithms, reducing schedule makespan by an average factor of $1.4\times$ on GPT AI workloads, $1.9\times$ on MoE AI workloads, and $2.4\times$ on standard benchmarks. Further, the makespans achieved by Spectra consistently approach newly derived lower bounds.

</details>


### [153] [Silicone Ethernet (SEth): a Nervous System for Robotic Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08292)
*Mengyao Liu,Dag Malstaf,Jonathan Oostvogels,Sam Michiels,Alexander Badri-Spröwitz,Danny Hughes*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: SEth是一种用于机器人触觉感知的无线解决方案，通过导电硅胶基板实现传感、通信和能量传输，无需电池和布线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉感知受限于嵌入式传感器的电缆布线和成本问题，需要无线解决方案来支持精细触觉感知

Method: 开发Silicone Ethernet (SEth)系统，使用无电池神经元节点嵌入导电硅胶基板，实现计算、通信、传感和能量收集

Result: SEth达到100kbps数据传输速率，接收功耗低于μW级，发射功耗mW级，支持电容触觉感知，可在1米范围内每秒传输数十条消息

Conclusion: SEth为软体机器人触觉感知提供了新的自由度，解决了传统有线传感系统的限制

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic touch sensing is essential for tasks such as robot-human interaction and the handling of hazardous materials. Yet, the sense of touch of robots is limited by the cost and complexity of routing cables to embedded sensors. This paper tackles this problem by contributing Silicone Ethernet (SEth), a wireless solution for touch sensing, communication and power transfer within a conductive silicone substrate. SEth~\emph{neurons} require no battery and deliver computation, communication, sensing and energy harvesting within a compact package. These neurons are placed into an undifferentiated conductive silicone substrate which may form the entire body of a soft robot, or an outer `skin' for hard robots. Our evaluation shows that SEth achieves data rates of 100\,kbps with sub-$μ$W receive and mW-scale transmit power. Exploiting the unique properties of the conductive silicone substrate, SEth provides prioritized traffic arbitration similar to that found in wired control networks such as CAN. The SEth network inherently supports capacitive touch and presence sensing and neurons can harvest sufficient energy to transmit 10s of messages per second at a range of 1\,m. Considered in sum, these features open new degrees of freedom in touch sensing for soft robots.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Scalable On-the-fly Transcoding for Adaptive Streaming of Dynamic Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08417)
*Michael Rudolph,Matthias De Fré,Finn Schnier,Tim Wauter,Amr Rizk*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 评估动态点云流媒体系统中实时转码的可扩展性，分析缓存和推测性转码对用户体验质量的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时转码可以减少动态点云序列的存储需求，并虚拟增加按需流媒体场景中的可用表示形式。然而，实时转码会给媒体提供商的架构带来额外工作负载。虽然V-PCC编码内容可以通过重新编码底层视频比特流进行高效转码，并能从硬件加速的视频编解码器实现中受益，但此类系统的可扩展性仍不明确。

Method: 引入并评估一个利用实时转码的动态点云流媒体系统。探索该系统在请求完成时间方面的可扩展性限制，特别评估感知用户体验质量。通过实证研究分析缓存和推测性转码如何显著减少转码负载。

Result: 缓存和推测性转码能够显著降低转码负载，使系统能够扩展到更高数量的同时在线客户端。

Conclusion: 实时转码在动态点云流媒体系统中具有可行性，通过缓存和推测性转码策略可以有效管理系统负载，提高系统可扩展性，从而支持更多同时在线用户。

Abstract: On-the-fly transcoding of dynamic point cloud sequences reduces storage requirements and virtually increases the number of available representations for on demand streaming scenarios. On-the-fly transcoding introduces, however, additional workload to media providers' infrastructure. While V-PCC encoded content can be efficiently transcoded by re-encoding the underlying video bitstreams, which greatly benefits from hardware-accelerated video codec implementations, the scalability of such a system remains unclear. In this work, we introduce and evaluate a dynamic point cloud streaming system that utilizes on-the-fly transcoding. We explore the limits of scalability of this system in terms of request fulfillment times, specifically evaluating the perceived user Quality of Experience. We empirically show how caching and speculative transcoding allow to significantly reduce transcoding loads, allowing to scale to a higher number of simultaneous clients.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Joint Inverse Learning of Cognitive Radar Perception and Perception-Action Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07051)
*Anoop C,Anup Aprem*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出IPFDDP算法，通过非参数贝叶斯方法联合估计认知雷达的感知状态和感知驱动策略，解决了现有方法需要已知其中一项的局限性，并集成主动探测实现更高效的逆学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子对抗方法在对抗性环境中假设已知认知雷达的感知状态或感知-行动策略之一，然后推断另一项，这种假设在实际对抗场景中不现实。需要开发能够同时推断两者且无需先验知识的逆学习框架。

Method: 提出基于依赖狄利克雷过程的逆粒子滤波算法（IPFDDP），使用依赖狄利克雷过程建模感知依赖的行动策略，将核基DDP推理嵌入贝叶斯逆粒子滤波框架，联合估计认知雷达的感知状态和感知-行动策略。

Result: IPFDDP在均方误差、KL散度和行动偏好识别准确率方面优于现有逆学习方法。贝叶斯框架自然量化推断的不确定性，集成主动探测后比随机探测平均减少40%的KL散度收敛时间。

Conclusion: IPFDDP为对抗性环境中的电子对抗提供了有效的逆学习框架，能够同时估计认知雷达的感知状态和策略，且通过不确定性量化支持主动探测策略，显著提升学习效率。

Abstract: Cognitive Radars (CRs) employ perception-action cycle to adapt their sensing and transmission strategies based on its' perception of the target kinematic states and mission objectives. This paper considers an inverse learning Electronic Counter Measure (ECM) that infers both the perception and perception-driven action policy of the adversarial CR's from the actions of the CR, i.e. the sensing and transmission actions taken by the CR. Existing frameworks, in the literature, assume the knowledge of either the perception or the perception-action policy and infer the other. However, this assumption is unrealistic in an adversarial setting. We address this gap by proposing an online, nonparametric Bayesian machine learning framework and developing the Inverse Particle Filter with Dependent Dirichlet Process (IPFDDP) algorithm, which characterizes the perception-dependent action policy using a Dependent Dirichlet Process (DDP) and embeds kernel-based DDP inference within a Bayesian inverse particle filtering framework to jointly estimate the CR's perception and perception-action policy. Extensive numerical simulations demonstrate that IPFDDP outperforms existing inverse learning methods in terms of mean squared error, Kullback-Leibler divergence between the estimated and true policy, and accuracy in identifying relative action preferences. Unlike the existing techniques, the proposed Bayesian formulation naturally quantifies uncertainty in inferred perception and perception-action policy, enabling active probing strategies for sample efficient inverse learning. Simulation results show that active probing integrated with IPFDDP achieves, on average, a 40% faster reduction in KL divergence compared to randomized probing.

</details>


### [156] [Demo: An RFSoC-Based Testbed for Over-the-Air Wireless Transceiver at Millimeter Wave Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07209)
*Jeet Tekchandani,Jai Mangal,Sumit J. Darak,Shobha Sundar Ram*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 基于AMD RFSoC和Sivers半导体模拟前端的毫米波硬件测试平台，支持29.8GHz频段的模拟波束成形通信


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波频段具有大带宽优势，但信号衰减严重，需要窄定向波束进行通信。现有系统需要验证实际环境中的性能

Method: 采用AMD RFSoC平台，ARM处理器实现OFDM基带物理层，FPGA实现数字前端，集成高速数据转换器。RFSoC输出与29.8GHz毫米波多天线模拟前端集成，支持模拟波束成形

Result: 实现了端到端空口通信，在真实无线信道中分析了射频损伤和波束失准对误码率的影响

Conclusion: 成功构建了毫米波模拟波束成形硬件测试平台，验证了在实际射频损伤和波束失准条件下的通信性能

Abstract: The millimeter wave (mmW) frequency spectrum has been explored recently for large bandwidth communication. At these frequencies, narrow directional beams are required for communication since the signal attenuation is high due to atmospheric absorption. This work presents an AMD RFSoC and Sivers Semiconductors analog front-end based hardware testbed capable of directional communication via analog beamforming at mmW. The proposed testbed comprises orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) based baseband physical layer and digital front-end on an ARM processor and field programmable gate array (FPGA), respectively, integrated with high-speed data converters of the RFSoC. The RFSoC output at sub-6GHz is integrated with a mmW multi-antenna analog-front end for over- the-air communication at 29.8 GHz. We demonstrate end-to-end communication over the air and present bit error rate (BER) analysis in the presence of radio frequency impairments and beam misalignments in real radio channels.

</details>


### [157] [Radar Enabled Adaptive Modulation for Millimeter Wave Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07216)
*Jai Mangal,Sumit Darak,Shobha Sundar Ram*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于雷达感知的调制方案选择方法，消除传统上下行反馈机制，在四种轨迹下实现54-209%的吞吐量提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISAC研究主要利用雷达感知进行波束识别，但调制方案选择仍依赖传统的耗时上下行反馈机制，需要更高效的调制方案选择方法

Method: 提出雷达使能的调制方案选择方案，利用雷达感知信息直接选择最优调制方案，无需传统反馈机制

Result: 在四种不同轨迹下测试，相比现有ISAC方法，吞吐量提升54-209%

Conclusion: 雷达使能的调制方案选择能显著提升ISAC系统性能，为6G高速车载通信等应用提供高效解决方案

Abstract: An integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) framework comprises radar sensing to enable reliable direction beam-based communication between a base station (BS) and mobile user (MU). The ISAC will be an integral part of 6G with potential applications for high-speed vehicular communications. Existing works have explored azimuth and Doppler velocity estimated via radar sensing for beam identification and identification in dynamic environments. In this work, we propose radar-enabled modulation scheme selection for ISAC, thereby eliminating conventional time-consuming downlink-uplink feedback-based modulation scheme selection. We have analyzed the performance of the proposed approach for four different trajectories and shown an improvement in throughput between 54-209% over state-of-the-art ISAC.

</details>


### [158] [Millimeter Wave Frontend for Integrated Sensing and Communication System Transceiver on Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07220)
*Jai Mangal,Kshitiz Joshi,Krishna Neel Reddy,Soumya Jain,Shobha Sundar Ram,Sumit J. Darak*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于IEEE 802.11ad的集成感知与通信（ISAC）基站收发器原型，通过共享波形、频谱和毫米波前端实现雷达感知与通信功能，消除传统波束对准开销，提升系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEEE 802.11ad标准采用模拟波束成形进行毫米波定向通信，但波束对准过程需要大量数据包交换，引入显著开销，降低整体吞吐量。现有ISAC方案虽能消除波束对准时间，但缺乏完整的端到端原型系统来评估实际性能。

Method: 设计并实现基于IEEE 802.11ad的ISAC基站收发器原型，将边缘数字基带硬件前端与Simulink建模的毫米波前端集成。雷达和通信功能共享相同波形、频谱和毫米波前端，具有共同的空间视场。雷达检测并定位移动用户，为后续定向通信提供支持。

Result: 通过系统链路预算和详细性能分析，评估了不同无线信道、目标运动、信噪比、硬件配置和损伤的影响。结果显示，即使在存在硬件损伤的情况下，基于802.11ad的ISAC系统相比理想毫米波前端的标准方案仍能提供34%的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 提出的端到端ISAC原型系统验证了集成感知与通信在毫米波频段的可行性，通过消除波束对准开销显著提升系统性能，为实际ISAC系统部署提供了重要参考。

Abstract: IEEE 802.11ad standard uses analog beamforming for high-speed directional communication with mobile user (MU) in the millimeter wave (mmWave) spectrum. However, the lengthy beam alignment procedures involving large data packets between the base station (BS) and the MU introduce considerable overhead, deteriorating the overall throughput. Prior works have proposed 802.11ad-based integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) BS transceivers to eliminate time-consuming beam alignment. Instead, the radar and communication functionalities use the same waveform, spectrum, and millimeter wave front end (MFE) with a common spatial field of view. The radar detects and localizes the MU, enabling the subsequent directional communication with the MU. This work proposes an end-to-end IEEE 802.11ad-based ISAC BS transceiver prototype, wherein the digital baseband hardware frontend on edge is integrated with a Simulink-based MFE. The proposed prototype facilitates a systematic link budget and detailed performance analysis for different wireless channels, target motions, signal-to-noise ratios, hardware configurations, and impairments. We also investigate how these impairments affect radar performance and, in turn, the communication metrics since the performances of both systems are uniquely interrelated in an ISAC system. Our results show that even with hardware impairments, the 802.11ad-based ISAC offers 34% higher throughput than the standard with an ideal MFE.

</details>


### [159] [A Low-Complexity PFA-Based Autofocus Algorithm for Automotive SAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07332)
*S. Hamed Javadi,André Bourdoux,Adnan Albaba,Hichem Sahli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种用于汽车合成孔径雷达的双层自动聚焦策略，结合极坐标格式算法和反投影算法，通过定位误差补偿提高图像质量同时保持低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动聚焦算法主要针对频域SAR技术设计，而汽车应用中常采用反投影算法以获得更少失真的图像，但缺乏针对时域算法的高效自动聚焦解决方案。

Method: 提出双层自动聚焦策略：第一层在PFA域使用新颖的定位误差补偿自动聚焦处理流程估计和校正定位误差；第二层将校正结果无缝传输到BPA，并扩展相位梯度自动聚焦技术来增强BPA的定位误差补偿效率。

Result: 通过真实汽车实验验证，所提流程实现了最先进的图像聚焦和分辨率，为计算高效的SAR成像设定了新基准。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了时域SAR算法高效自动聚焦解决方案的空白，通过PFA与BPA的集成策略，在保持低复杂度的同时实现了高质量的SAR成像。

Abstract: Radars provide robust perception of vehicle surroundings by effectively functioning in poor light and adverse weather conditions. Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) algorithms are employed to address the limited angular resolution of radars by enlarging antenna aperture size synthetically as the radar moves. An autofocus algorithm is essential to improve the SAR image quality by compensating for errors mainly caused by inaccurate radar localization. Existing autofocus algorithms are mostly tailored for the frequency domain SAR techniques which are prevalent in aviation and spaceborne applications thanks to their lower complexity in large data processing. However, in the automotive context, the backprojection algorithm (BPA) is often preferred since it provides less distorted images at the cost of more complexity. Addressing the gap in efficient autofocus solutions for time-domain algorithms, this paper introduces a dual-layered autofocus strategy that integrates the Polar Format Algorithm (PFA) with BPA. The first layer employs a novel Localization Error Compensation Autofocus (LECA) processing pipeline to estimate and correct the localization errors within the PFA domain, leveraging its computational efficiency. The second layer seamlessly transfers these corrections to BPA, enabling high-quality SAR imaging while maintaining low complexity. Additionally, the strategy extends Phase Gradient Autofocus (PGA) techniques to enhance the efficiency of localization error compensation for BPA. Validated through real-world automotive experiments, the proposed pipeline delivers state-of-the-art image focus and resolution, setting a new benchmark for computationally efficient SAR imaging.

</details>


### [160] [GP Bandit-Assisted Two-Stage Sparse Phase Retrieval for Amplitude-Only Near-Field Beam Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07477)
*Zijun Wang,Shawn Tsai,Ye Hu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出两阶段稀疏相位恢复框架，用于多径信道中的仅幅度近场波束训练，通过自适应支撑发现和子空间细化提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G中极大规模天线阵列(ELAA)引入显著近场效应，需要鲁棒的近场波束训练策略。由于硬件损伤(如相位噪声、频率偏移)经常破坏信号相位，仅幅度信道恢复成为相干波束训练的关键替代方案。现有近场幅度训练方法常假设简化的视距条件，而远场相位恢复方法缺乏优化训练效率所需的感知灵活性，且受限于平面波模型，不适用于近场传播。

Method: 提出两阶段稀疏相位恢复框架：第一阶段在标准2D DFT波束空间上执行自适应支撑发现，利用近场波束模式诱导的物理引导先验；第二阶段将感知和稀疏相位恢复限制在学习的子空间内，从而细化信道估计。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的自适应流水线持续优于非自适应基线方法，在低信噪比下将波束成形增益提高超过70%。

Conclusion: 该两阶段稀疏相位恢复框架为多径环境中的仅幅度近场波束训练提供了有效的解决方案，通过自适应支撑发现和子空间细化显著提升了性能，特别适用于存在硬件损伤的实际场景。

Abstract: The transition to Extremely Large Antenna Arrays (ELAA) in 6G introduces significant near-field effects, necessitating robust near-field beam training strategies in multi-path environments. Because signal phases are frequently compromised by hardware impairments such as phase noise and frequency offsets, amplitude-only channel recovery is a critical alternative to coherent beam training. However, existing near-field amplitude-based training methods often assume simplistic line-of-sight conditions. Conversely, far-field phase retrieval (PR) methods lack the sensing flexibility required to optimize training efficiency and are fundamentally limited by plane-wave models, making them ill-suited for near-field propagation. We propose a two-stage sparse PR framework for amplitude-only near-field beam training in multipath channels. Stage I performs adaptive support discovery on the standard 2D DFT beamspace by exploiting a physics-guided prior induced by near-field beam patterns. Stage II then refines the channel estimate by restricting sensing and sparse PR to the learned subspace. Numerical results show that the proposed adaptive pipeline consistently outperforms non-adaptive baselines, improving beamforming gain by over 70% at low SNR.

</details>


### [161] [MetaSort: An Accelerated Approach for Non-uniform Compression and Few-shot Classification of Neural Spike Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07602)
*Luca M. Meyer,Majid Zamani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出MetaSort算法，同时解决尖峰分类和压缩问题，通过自适应电平穿越算法压缩尖峰形状，利用潜在特征表示进行分类，并采用元迁移学习增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有尖峰排序研究通常将尖峰分类和压缩作为独立问题处理，缺乏统一的解决方案。本文旨在开发一种能同时处理这两个问题的算法，为超低功耗片上实现提供可能。

Method: 提出MetaSort算法：1) 使用新颖的自适应电平穿越算法进行高保真尖峰形状压缩；2) 利用潜在特征表示处理分类问题；3) 通过元迁移学习框架同时利用数据的几何信息，确保算法的鲁棒性和判别性。

Result: 使用体内尖峰数据进行实证实验，结果表明MetaSort表现出优异的性能，验证了其潜在应用价值，为超低功耗片上实现提供了有力支持。

Conclusion: MetaSort成功统一了尖峰分类和压缩问题，通过自适应压缩、潜在特征表示和元迁移学习的结合，实现了高性能的尖峰排序，为未来超低功耗神经接口设备的开发奠定了基础。

Abstract: Many previous works in spike sorting study spike classification and compression independently. In this paper, a novel algorithm is proposed called MetaSort to address these two problems. To deal with compression, a novel adaptive level crossing algorithm is proposed to approximate spike shapes with high fidelity. Meanwhile, the latent feature representation is used to handle the classification problem. Besides, to guarantee MetaSort is robust and discriminative, the geometric information of data is exploited simultaneously in the proposed framework by meta-transfer learning. Empirical experiments with in-vivo spike data demonstrate that MetaSort delivers promising performance, highlighting its potential and motivating continued development toward an ultra-low-power, on-chip implementation.

</details>


### [162] [Energy-Aware Multi-Exit TinyML for Smart Zero-Energy Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08047)
*Shahab Jahanbazi,Mateen Ashraf,Lieven De Strycker,Jeroen Famaey,Onel L. A. Lopez*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于零能耗设备（ZED）的统一智能管理框架，通过多出口点自适应推理和能量感知电路，在保持检测精度的同时显著降低能耗约29.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能和自主系统的发展，需要在边缘设备上直接执行智能计算。这对于零能耗设备（ZED）尤其具有挑战性，因为需要同时解决内存、能量可用性和推理精度的严格约束。现有方法难以在保持精度的同时有效管理能量消耗。

Method: 设计、训练和部署用于人员检测的微型机器学习（TinyML）模型。采用统一架构：1）内存中存储单一模型，但通过多个出口点实现自适应推理，使计算量随输入难度动态调整；2）引入辅助能量感知电路，根据可用能量动态调节系统运行，防止电源故障。

Result: 与最先进的能量感知单出口TinyML方法相比，所提方法实现了约29.6%的能耗降低。该方法在保持检测精度的同时，显著减少了能量消耗，实现了低能耗推理用于简单实例，高精度推理用于困难案例的选择性应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为ZED平台实现准确且能量高效的智能提供了有吸引力的解决方案。通过自适应推理和能量感知电路的结合，有效解决了ZED在内存、能量和精度方面的多重约束，为边缘智能设备的发展提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The proliferation of smart and autonomous systems has motivated a shift toward executing intelligence directly on edge devices. This shift becomes particularly challenging for zero-energy devices (ZEDs), where severe constraints on memory, energy availability, and inference accuracy must be addressed simultaneously. In this paper, we present a unified approach to managing these constraints for smart ZEDs. Specifically, we design, train, and deploy a tiny machine learning (TinyML) model for person detection on a ZED. The proposed architecture stores a single model in memory while enabling adaptive inference through multiple exit points, allowing computational effort to scale with input difficulty. As a result, low-energy inference is performed for easy instances, while higher-precision inference is selectively employed for harder cases. This strategy significantly reduces energy consumption without sacrificing detection accuracy. Furthermore, to enhance device autonomy and prevent power failures, we introduce auxiliary energy-aware circuits that dynamically regulate system operation based on available energy. Compared with a state-of-the-art energy-aware single-exit TinyML approach, the proposed method achieves an energy consumption reduction of approximately $29.6\%$. Overall, the proposed framework is appealing for enabling accurate and energy-efficient intelligence on ZED platforms.

</details>


### [163] [Chirp-Based Multi-Device Ambient Backscatter Communication and Sensing Enabled by OFDM-AFDM Symbiotic Radio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08109)
*Katia Abtouche,Fikiri Salum Uledi,Ayoub Ammar Boudjelal,Muhammad Bilal Janjua,Zinat Behdad,Cicek Cavdar,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于OFDM和AFDM波形正交性的共生无线电系统，实现主通信信号与反向散射通信信号在同一频谱内无干扰共存，支持同时传感和数据传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反向散射通信系统面临直接链路干扰和反向散射设备间干扰问题，需要在不影响主链路性能的前提下实现低功耗、抗干扰的集成传感与反向散射通信。

Method: 利用OFDM和AFDM信号的波形域正交性，采用基于啁啾的AFDM波形，将反向散射传输嵌入仿射域，同时为每个反向散射设备分配不同的仿射域偏移来抑制设备间干扰。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方案能有效抑制干扰而不影响主链路误码率，同时改善了反向散射通信的漏检概率性能，实现了信号域无干扰共存。

Conclusion: 所提出的ISABC系统通过波形正交性实现了主通信与反向散射通信的无干扰共存，为未来低功耗、抗干扰系统提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel symbiotic radio system for integrated sensing and backscatter communication (ISABC) technique that enables signal-domain interference-free coexistence of the primary communication signal and the backscatter communication (BC) signal within the same spectrum. The proposed system design allows simultaneous backscatter devices (BDs) sensing and data transmission without mutual interference by exploiting waveform-domain orthogonality between orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) and affine frequency domain multiplexing (AFDM) signals. Specifically, a chirp-based AFDM waveform is adopted due to its inherent processing gain, which enhances the detectability and reliability of the weak backscatter signal while simultaneously supporting high-resolution sensing. Unlike conventional methods that attempt to suppress direct-link interference (DLI), this approach embeds the backscatter transmission within the affine domain while maintaining reliable OFDM-based primary communication. Furthermore, by assigning distinct affine-domain shifts to each backscatter device, the proposed framework inherently suppresses inter-backscatter device interference (IBDI). Comprehensive simulation results demonstrate that the proposed coexistence scheme effectively mitigates interference without affecting the error rate of the primary link and improves the miss-detection probability performance of the BC, making it a promising candidate for future low-power and interferenceresilient systems.

</details>


### [164] [Instantaneous Frequency Estimation in Noisy Multicomponent Signals with Interfering Modes Based on Prony Method and Spline Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08198)
*Basile Dubois-Bonnaire,Sylvain Meignen,Kévin Polisano*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于样条近似的新颖瞬时频率估计器，用于处理含噪声多分量信号中紧密模式的情况，通过结合Prony技术和样条近似来消除估计中的振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理含噪声多分量信号中紧密模式在时频平面上的瞬时频率估计问题。Prony方法虽然可以适应这种情况，但模式间的干扰会导致估计结果产生振荡，需要解决这一问题。

Method: 首先研究Prony方法估计中振荡的性质，然后提出基于样条近似的算法来消除这些振荡。将Prony技术与样条近似相结合，用于含噪声多分量信号中紧密模式的瞬时频率估计。

Result: 在各种情况下进行的数值应用表明，将Prony技术与样条近似相结合，对于含噪声多分量信号中紧密模式的瞬时频率估计具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 提出的混合Prony技术和样条近似的算法能有效消除瞬时频率估计中的振荡，为处理含噪声多分量信号中紧密模式提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel estimator of the instantaneous frequencies (IFs) of the modes making up multicomponent signals (MCSs). We are particularly interested in dealing with noisy MCSs containing close modes in the time-frequency plane. Though it is possible to adapt Prony approach to estimate IFs in such situations, interference between the modes generates oscillations in the obtained estimations. After having investigated the nature of these oscillations, we propose an algorithm to remove these in IFs estimation, based on spline approximation. Numerical applications in various situations illustrate the benefit of mixing Prony technique with spline approximation for IF estimation in noisy MCSs containing close modes.

</details>


### [165] [Fluid Antennas Meet Intelligent Surfaces: Security Analysis of NOMA Systems Under Hardware Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08244)
*Tuo Wu,Jianchao Zheng,Xiazhi Lai,Maged Elkashlan,Hyundong Shin,Naofal Al-Dhahir*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在硬件损伤条件下，流体天线系统与可重构智能表面结合的NOMA系统安全性能，建立了考虑硬件损伤影响的平均安全误块率分析框架，揭示了硬件质量对系统性能的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统与可重构智能表面的结合为无线通信安全提供了前所未有的机会，但硬件损伤对这种组合的实际影响尚未得到充分探索。论文旨在研究在现实硬件约束下，流体天线与智能表面结合的NOMA系统的安全性能。

Method: 开发了全面的分析框架，捕捉自适应空间分集、智能信号反射和硬件引起失真之间的复杂相互作用。采用新颖的分段线性近似和块相关模型，推导出平均安全误块率的可处理表达式，揭示硬件损伤施加的基本性能限制。

Result: 分析表明，虽然流体天线与智能表面的协同为安全增强提供了显著的自由度，但实际的硬件缺陷会创建性能上限，无论空间分集增益如何。理论框架揭示了系统复杂性与可实现安全性能之间的关键设计权衡，表明硬件质量成为实现FAS-RIS架构全部潜力的决定性因素。

Conclusion: 硬件损伤对FAS-RIS-NOMA系统的安全性能有根本性影响，硬件质量决定了系统性能上限。研究为在实际硬件限制下有效平衡流体智能协作优势与约束的NOMA系统设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The revolutionary convergence of fluid antenna systems (FAS) and reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) creates unprecedented opportunities for secure wireless communications, yet the practical implications of hardware impairments on this promising combination remain largely unexplored. This paper investigates the security performance of non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) systems when fluid antennas (FAs) meet intelligent surfaces under realistic hardware constraints. We develop a comprehensive analytical framework that captures the complex interplay between adaptive spatial diversity, intelligent signal reflection, and hardware-induced distortions in short-packet communications. Through novel piecewise linear approximations and block-correlation models, we derive tractable expressions for average secure block error rate (BLER) that reveal fundamental performance limits imposed by hardware impairments. Our analysis demonstrates that while the synergy between FAs and intelligent surfaces offers remarkable degrees of freedom for security enhancement, practical hardware imperfections create performance ceilings that persist regardless of spatial diversity gains. The theoretical framework exposes critical design trade-offs between system complexity and achievable security performance, showing that hardware quality becomes a decisive factor in realizing the full potential of FAS-RIS architectures. Extensive simulations validate our analytical insights and provide practical design guidelines for implementing secure NOMA systems that effectively balance the benefits of fluid-intelligent cooperation against the constraints of realistic hardware limitations.

</details>


### [166] [Mitigating Mixed-field Interference in Near-field and Far-field Communications: An Antenna Selection Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08389)
*Tianyu Liu,Changsheng You,Chao Zhou,Mingjiang Wu,Ming-Min Zhao,Zhaocheng Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出天线选择传输框架抑制混合近远场干扰，通过联合天线选择和功率分配优化和速率，在MRT模拟波束成形下实现干扰抑制与阵列增益的平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合近远场系统中，近场与远场信道的非正交性会导致严重的用户间干扰，降低速率性能。传统全阵列最大比传输(MRT)模拟波束成形无法有效解决此问题，需要不改变天线结构的干扰抑制方案

Method: 1) 针对两用户场景分析天线选择机制，揭示干扰抑制与阵列增益增强的权衡关系；2) 多用户场景下提出基于惩罚对偶分解(PDD)的两层优化框架，结合块坐标下降(BCD)和逐次凸逼近(SCA)技术；3) 提出基于干扰抑制准则的低复杂度天线去激活策略

Result: 理论分析表明仅去激活少量天线即可显著抑制混合场干扰而不严重损失阵列增益。数值结果显示所提方案在干扰抑制和阵列增益损失间取得良好平衡，性能优于多种基线方案

Conclusion: 天线选择传输框架能有效抑制混合近远场系统中的干扰，通过优化天线选择和功率分配实现显著的性能提升，为混合场通信系统提供了实用的干扰管理方案

Abstract: In mixed near-field and far-field systems, the nonorthogonality between near-field and far-field channels may cause severe inter-user interference and hence degrade rate performance, when the analog beamforming is designed based on the low-complexity full-array maximum ratio transmission (MRT). To tackle this issue, we propose in this paper an antenna selection-based transmission framework to effectively suppress mixed-field interference without mechanically altering antenna structures. To this end, an optimization problem is formulated to maximize the sum-rate of mixed-field systems, by jointly designing antenna selection and power allocation under the MRT-based analog beamforming. As the problem is non-convex and generally difficult to solve optimally, we first consider a typical two-user scenario to obtain useful insights. Interestingly, we analytically show that the strong mixed-field interference can be substantially mitigated by deactivating only a small portion of antennas, yet without compromising array gains too much. Moreover, an inherent tradeoff is revealed in antenna selection between interference suppression and array-gain enhancement, based on which a suboptimal number of deactivated antennas for achieving the maximum sum-rate is obtained. Next, for the general multi-user case, we develop an efficient penalty dual decomposition (PDD)-based two-layer framework to obtain its high quality solution by using the block coordinate descent (BCD) and successive convex approximation (SCA) techniques. To further reduce the computational complexity, a low-complexity antenna deactivation strategy is proposed capitalizing on an interference suppression criterion. Last, numerical results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves a favorable trade-off between interference suppression and array gain loss, hence achieving significant performance gains over various baseline schemes.

</details>


### [167] [Subspace Fusion Sensing for Cooperative ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08419)
*Yining Xu,Cunhua Pan,Jun Tang,Hong Ren,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种用于协作式集成感知与通信的子空间融合感知算法，通过张量展开构建等效虚拟天线阵列，实现无需数据关联的分布式AP融合感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作式集成感知与通信系统中，需要有效融合来自分布式接入点的感知信息，传统方法存在数据关联复杂、性能受限等问题，需要开发更高效的融合感知算法

Method: 1) 将来自多个AP的接收信号堆叠为三阶张量，通过张量展开构建等效虚拟天线阵列；2) 基于EVA阵列开发无需数据关联的子空间融合感知算法；3) 推导Cramer-Rao下界作为性能基准

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提算法相比传统技术具有更好的性能表现，验证了算法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的子空间融合感知算法能够有效解决协作式集成感知与通信中的分布式信息融合问题，无需复杂的数据关联过程，具有较好的性能表现

Abstract: This paper proposes a subspace fusion sensing algorithm for cooperative integrated sensing and communication. First, we stack the received signals from access points (APs) into a third-order tensor and construct the equivalent virtual antenna (EVA) array via tensor unfolding. Then, a data association-free subspace-based fusion sensing algorithm is developed utilizing the EVA arrays from distributed APs. A derivation of Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) is also presented. Finally, simulation results validate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm compared to traditional techniques.

</details>


### [168] [Graph Based Semantic Encoder Decoder Framework for Task Oriented Communications in Connected Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08438)
*Soheyb Ribouh,Phil Polo Ditsia Di Ngoma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于图的语义编码器-解码器（GBSED）架构，用于车联网中的任务导向通信，通过场景图捕捉道路实体关系，实现高达99%的压缩率，同时保持下游任务的高语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通信范式传输原始比特，导致带宽消耗过大且无法保持传输信息的语义相关性。车联网（CAV）需要可靠高效的通信框架来支持安全关键和任务导向应用，如碰撞避免、协同感知和交通风险评估。

Method: 提出图基语义编码器-解码器（GBSED）架构：编码器利用场景图捕捉道路实体的空间和语义关系，结合语义压缩算法将提取的图表示大小减少高达99%（相比原始图像）；解码器重建任务相关表示而非原始数据。该框架集成到MIMO OFDM物理层系统中进行评估。

Result: 在SNR高于10dB时语义保真度超过0.9；风险评估预测成功率较高；在3GPP CDL信道下具有改进的鲁棒性；实现显著的压缩增益。证实该语义通信框架是未来6G系统的有前景解决方案。

Conclusion: 提出的基于图的语义编码器-解码器架构能够显著减少通信开销，同时保持高语义保真度，为车联网中的任务导向通信提供了有效解决方案，适合未来6G系统应用。

Abstract: Connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs) require reliable and efficient communication frameworks to support safety critical and task-oriented applications such as collision avoidance, cooperative perception, and traffic risk assessment. Traditional communication paradigms, which focus on transmitting raw bits, often incur excessive bandwidth consumption and fail to preserve the semantic relevance of transmitted information. To bridge this gap, we propose a Graph-Based Semantic Encoder-Decoder (GBSED) architecture tailored for task-oriented communications in CAV networks. The encoder leverages scene graphs to capture spatial and semantic relationships among road entities, combined with a semantic compression algorithm that reduces the size of the extracted graph based representations by up to 99% compared to raw images, while the decoder reconstructs task relevant representations rather than raw data. This design enables a significant reduction in communication overhead while maintaining high semantic fidelity, exceeding 0.9 at SNR levels above 10dB, for downstream vehicular tasks. We evaluate the proposed framework through simulations in autonomous driving scenarios, where the semantic encoder and decoder are integrated into a MIMO OFDM physical layer system. The results demonstrate high prediction success rates for risk assessment, improved robustness under the 3GPP CDL channel, and significant compression gains, confirming that the proposed semantic communication framework is a promising solution for future 6G systems.

</details>


### [169] [Multi-Mode Pinching-Antenna Systems: Mode Selection or Mode Combining?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08472)
*Xiaoxia Xu,Xidong Mu,Yuanwei Liu,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多模式夹持天线系统(PASS)，通过介质波导传输多个正交模式信号并由夹持天线辐射，实现模式域复用以支持高效多用户通信，提出了模式选择和模式组合两种协议，并开发了基于粒子群优化的KKT参数化波束赋形算法进行联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单模式夹持天线系统在支持多用户通信时存在频谱效率限制，需要开发能够同时传输多个正交模式信号的多模式系统，以提高通信容量并支持高效的多用户通信。

Method: 提出多模式夹持天线系统，支持介质波导中多个正交模式的传输和辐射；设计了模式选择和模式组合两种操作协议；构建了和速率最大化问题，联合优化发射波束赋形、夹持天线位置和传播常数；开发了基于粒子群优化的KKT参数化波束赋形算法，利用KKT条件解指导搜索以降低搜索空间并加速收敛。

Result: 数值结果表明：1）即使采用简单的均匀模式组合设计，多模式PASS系统也显著优于传统单模式PASS和混合波束赋形系统；2）模式组合实现高频谱效率，而模式选择以较低的硬件复杂度近似其性能。

Conclusion: 多模式夹持天线系统通过模式域复用有效提升了多用户通信性能，提出的两种操作协议在频谱效率和硬件复杂度之间提供了灵活权衡，所开发的优化算法能够有效解决高度非凸的联合优化问题。

Abstract: This letter investigates multi-mode pinching antenna systems (PASS), where signals of multiple orthogonal modes can be transmitted within a dielectric waveguide and radiated by pinching antennas (PAs). This enables mode-domain multiplexing for efficient multi-user communications using a single waveguide. In particular, two operating protocols are proposed, namely mode selection and mode combining. Mode selection enforces each PA to predominantly radiate signal power of one single mode, while mode combining allows each PA to flexibly radiate power of multiple modes. Based on the two protocols, a sum rate maximization problem is formulated for multi-mode PASS-enabled multi-user downlink communications, where the transmit beamforming, PA positions, and PA propagation constants are jointly optimized. To address this rapidly oscillating and highly nonconvex problem, a particle swarm optimization (PSO) based Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT)-parameterized beamforming (PSO- KPBF) algorithm is proposed. KKT-conditioned solutions are exploited to guide the swarm search, thus reducing the search space and achieving fast convergence. Numerical results demonstrate that: 1) Even using a simple uniform mode-combining design, the multi-mode PASS significantly outperform conventional single-mode PASS and hybrid beamforming systems; and 2) Mode combining achieves high spectral efficiency, while mode selection approximates its performance with a lower hardware complexity. Code is released at https://github.com/xiaoxiaxusummer/multi_mode_pinching_antenna

</details>
